   28|       |//! Represents an in-memory input byte stream.
   29|       |/*!
   30|       |    This class is mainly for being wrapped by EncodedInputStream or AutoUTFInputStream.
   31|       |
   32|       |    It is similar to FileReadBuffer but the source is an in-memory buffer instead of a file.
   33|       |
   34|       |    Differences between MemoryStream and StringStream:
   35|       |    1. StringStream has encoding but MemoryStream is a byte stream.
   36|       |    2. MemoryStream needs size of the source buffer and the buffer don't need to be null terminated. StringStream assume null-terminated string as source.
   37|       |    3. MemoryStream supports Peek4() for encoding detection. StringStream is specified with an encoding so it should not have Peek4().
   38|       |    \note implements Stream concept
   39|       |*/
   40|       |struct MemoryStream {
   41|       |    typedef char Ch; // byte
   42|       |
   43|      0|    MemoryStream(const Ch *src, size_t size) : src_(src), begin_(src), end_(src + size), size_(size) {}
   44|       |
   45|      0|    Ch Peek() const { return CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(src_ == end_) ? '\0' : *src_; }
   46|      0|    Ch Take() { return CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(src_ == end_) ? '\0' : *src_++; }
   47|      0|    size_t Tell() const { return static_cast<size_t>(src_ - begin_); }
   48|       |
   49|      0|    Ch* PutBegin() { CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_ASSERT(false); return 0; }
   50|      0|    void Put(Ch) { CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_ASSERT(false); }
   51|      0|    void Flush() { CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_ASSERT(false); }
   52|      0|    size_t PutEnd(Ch*) { CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_ASSERT(false); return 0; }
   53|       |
   54|       |    // For encoding detection only.
   55|      0|    const Ch* Peek4() const {
   56|      0|        return Tell() + 4 <= size_ ? src_ : 0;
   57|      0|    }
   58|       |
   59|       |    const Ch* src_;     //!< Current read position.
   60|       |    const Ch* begin_;   //!< Original head of the string.
   61|       |    const Ch* end_;     //!< End of stream.
   62|       |    size_t size_;       //!< Size of the stream.
   63|       |};
   64|       |
   65|       |CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_NAMESPACE_END

  271|      0|}
  272|       |
  273|      0|inline const char* SkipWhitespace(const char* p, const char* end) {
  274|      0|    while (p != end && (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t'))
  275|      0|        ++p;

  277|      0|}
  278|       |
  279|       |#ifdef CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_SSE42
  280|       |//! Skip whitespace with SSE 4.2 pcmpistrm instruction, testing 16 8-byte characters at once.
  281|       |inline const char *SkipWhitespace_SIMD(const char* p) {
  282|       |    // Fast return for single non-whitespace
  283|       |    if (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t')
  284|       |        ++p;
  285|       |    else
  286|       |        return p;
  287|       |
  288|       |    // 16-byte align to the next boundary
  289|       |    const char* nextAligned = reinterpret_cast<const char*>((reinterpret_cast<size_t>(p) + 15) & static_cast<size_t>(~15));
  290|       |    while (p != nextAligned)
  291|       |        if (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t')
  292|       |            ++p;
  293|       |        else
  294|       |            return p;
  295|       |
  296|       |    // The rest of string using SIMD
  297|       |    static const char whitespace[16] = " \n\r\t";
  298|       |    const __m128i w = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespace[0]));
  299|       |
  300|       |    for (;; p += 16) {
  301|       |        const __m128i s = _mm_load_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(p));
  302|       |        const int r = _mm_cmpistri(w, s, _SIDD_UBYTE_OPS | _SIDD_CMP_EQUAL_ANY | _SIDD_LEAST_SIGNIFICANT | _SIDD_NEGATIVE_POLARITY);
  303|       |        if (r != 16)    // some of characters is non-whitespace
  304|       |            return p + r;
  305|       |    }
  306|       |}
  307|       |
  308|       |inline const char *SkipWhitespace_SIMD(const char* p, const char* end) {
  309|       |    // Fast return for single non-whitespace
  310|       |    if (p != end && (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t'))
  311|       |        ++p;
  312|       |    else
  313|       |        return p;
  314|       |
  315|       |    // The middle of string using SIMD
  316|       |    static const char whitespace[16] = " \n\r\t";
  317|       |    const __m128i w = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespace[0]));
  318|       |
  319|       |    for (; p <= end - 16; p += 16) {
  320|       |        const __m128i s = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(p));
  321|       |        const int r = _mm_cmpistri(w, s, _SIDD_UBYTE_OPS | _SIDD_CMP_EQUAL_ANY | _SIDD_LEAST_SIGNIFICANT | _SIDD_NEGATIVE_POLARITY);
  322|       |        if (r != 16)    // some of characters is non-whitespace
  323|       |            return p + r;
  324|       |    }
  325|       |
  326|       |    return SkipWhitespace(p, end);
  327|       |}
  328|       |
  329|       |#elif defined(CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_SSE2)
  330|       |
  331|       |//! Skip whitespace with SSE2 instructions, testing 16 8-byte characters at once.
  332|       |inline const char *SkipWhitespace_SIMD(const char* p) {
  333|       |    // Fast return for single non-whitespace
  334|       |    if (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t')
  335|       |        ++p;
  336|       |    else
  337|       |        return p;
  338|       |
  339|       |    // 16-byte align to the next boundary
  340|       |    const char* nextAligned = reinterpret_cast<const char*>((reinterpret_cast<size_t>(p) + 15) & static_cast<size_t>(~15));
  341|       |    while (p != nextAligned)
  342|       |        if (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t')
  343|       |            ++p;
  344|       |        else
  345|       |            return p;
  346|       |
  347|       |    // The rest of string
  348|      0|    #define C16(c) { c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c }
  349|       |    static const char whitespaces[4][16] = { C16(' '), C16('\n'), C16('\r'), C16('\t') };
  350|       |    #undef C16
  351|       |
  352|       |    const __m128i w0 = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespaces[0][0]));
  353|       |    const __m128i w1 = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespaces[1][0]));
  354|       |    const __m128i w2 = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespaces[2][0]));
  355|       |    const __m128i w3 = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespaces[3][0]));
  356|       |
  357|       |    for (;; p += 16) {
  358|       |        const __m128i s = _mm_load_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(p));
  359|       |        __m128i x = _mm_cmpeq_epi8(s, w0);
  360|       |        x = _mm_or_si128(x, _mm_cmpeq_epi8(s, w1));
  361|       |        x = _mm_or_si128(x, _mm_cmpeq_epi8(s, w2));
  362|       |        x = _mm_or_si128(x, _mm_cmpeq_epi8(s, w3));
  363|       |        unsigned short r = static_cast<unsigned short>(~_mm_movemask_epi8(x));
  364|       |        if (r != 0) {   // some of characters may be non-whitespace
  365|       |#ifdef _MSC_VER         // Find the index of first non-whitespace
  366|       |            unsigned long offset;
  367|       |            _BitScanForward(&offset, r);
  368|       |            return p + offset;
  369|       |#else
  370|       |            return p + __builtin_ffs(r) - 1;
  371|       |#endif
  372|       |        }
  373|       |    }
  374|       |}
  375|       |
  376|       |inline const char *SkipWhitespace_SIMD(const char* p, const char* end) {
  377|       |    // Fast return for single non-whitespace
  378|       |    if (p != end && (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t'))
  379|       |        ++p;
  380|       |    else
  381|       |        return p;
  382|       |
  383|       |    // The rest of string
  384|      0|    #define C16(c) { c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c, c }
  385|       |    static const char whitespaces[4][16] = { C16(' '), C16('\n'), C16('\r'), C16('\t') };
  386|       |    #undef C16
  387|       |
  388|       |    const __m128i w0 = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespaces[0][0]));
  389|       |    const __m128i w1 = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespaces[1][0]));
  390|       |    const __m128i w2 = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespaces[2][0]));
  391|       |    const __m128i w3 = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&whitespaces[3][0]));
  392|       |
  393|       |    for (; p <= end - 16; p += 16) {
  394|       |        const __m128i s = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(p));

  396|       |        x = _mm_or_si128(x, _mm_cmpeq_epi8(s, w1));

  401|       |#ifdef _MSC_VER         // Find the index of first non-whitespace
  402|       |            unsigned long offset;
  403|       |            _BitScanForward(&offset, r);
  404|       |            return p + offset;
  405|       |#else
  406|       |            return p + __builtin_ffs(r) - 1;
  407|       |#endif
  408|       |        }

  413|       |
  414|       |#elif defined(CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_NEON)
  415|       |
  416|       |//! Skip whitespace with ARM Neon instructions, testing 16 8-byte characters at once.
  417|       |inline const char *SkipWhitespace_SIMD(const char* p) {
  418|       |    // Fast return for single non-whitespace
  419|       |    if (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t')
  420|       |        ++p;
  421|       |    else
  422|       |        return p;
  423|       |
  424|       |    // 16-byte align to the next boundary
  425|       |    const char* nextAligned = reinterpret_cast<const char*>((reinterpret_cast<size_t>(p) + 15) & static_cast<size_t>(~15));
  426|       |    while (p != nextAligned)
  427|       |        if (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t')
  428|       |            ++p;
  429|       |        else
  430|       |            return p;
  431|       |
  432|       |    const uint8x16_t w0 = vmovq_n_u8(' ');
  433|       |    const uint8x16_t w1 = vmovq_n_u8('\n');
  434|       |    const uint8x16_t w2 = vmovq_n_u8('\r');
  435|       |    const uint8x16_t w3 = vmovq_n_u8('\t');
  436|       |
  437|       |    for (;; p += 16) {
  438|       |        const uint8x16_t s = vld1q_u8(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(p));
  439|       |        uint8x16_t x = vceqq_u8(s, w0);
  440|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, w1));
  441|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, w2));
  442|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, w3));
  443|       |
  444|       |        x = vmvnq_u8(x);                       // Negate
  445|       |        x = vrev64q_u8(x);                     // Rev in 64
  446|       |        uint64_t low = vgetq_lane_u64(reinterpret_cast<uint64x2_t>(x), 0);   // extract
  447|       |        uint64_t high = vgetq_lane_u64(reinterpret_cast<uint64x2_t>(x), 1);  // extract
  448|       |
  449|       |        if (low == 0) {
  450|       |            if (high != 0) {
  451|       |                int lz =__builtin_clzll(high);;
  452|       |                return p + 8 + (lz >> 3);
  453|       |            }
  454|       |        } else {
  455|       |            int lz = __builtin_clzll(low);;
  456|       |            return p + (lz >> 3);
  457|       |        }
  458|       |    }
  459|       |}
  460|       |
  461|       |inline const char *SkipWhitespace_SIMD(const char* p, const char* end) {
  462|       |    // Fast return for single non-whitespace
  463|       |    if (p != end && (*p == ' ' || *p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t'))
  464|       |        ++p;
  465|       |    else
  466|       |        return p;
  467|       |
  468|       |    const uint8x16_t w0 = vmovq_n_u8(' ');
  469|       |    const uint8x16_t w1 = vmovq_n_u8('\n');
  470|       |    const uint8x16_t w2 = vmovq_n_u8('\r');
  471|       |    const uint8x16_t w3 = vmovq_n_u8('\t');
  472|       |
  473|       |    for (; p <= end - 16; p += 16) {
  474|       |        const uint8x16_t s = vld1q_u8(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(p));
  475|       |        uint8x16_t x = vceqq_u8(s, w0);
  476|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, w1));
  477|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, w2));
  478|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, w3));
  479|       |
  480|       |        x = vmvnq_u8(x);                       // Negate
  481|       |        x = vrev64q_u8(x);                     // Rev in 64
  482|       |        uint64_t low = vgetq_lane_u64(reinterpret_cast<uint64x2_t>(x), 0);   // extract
  483|       |        uint64_t high = vgetq_lane_u64(reinterpret_cast<uint64x2_t>(x), 1);  // extract
  484|       |
  485|       |        if (low == 0) {
  486|       |            if (high != 0) {
  487|       |                int lz = __builtin_clzll(high);
  488|       |                return p + 8 + (lz >> 3);
  489|       |            }
  490|       |        } else {
  491|       |            int lz = __builtin_clzll(low);
  492|       |            return p + (lz >> 3);
  493|       |        }
  494|       |    }
  495|       |
  496|       |    return SkipWhitespace(p, end);

  518|       |// GenericReader
  519|       |
  520|       |//! SAX-style JSON parser. Use \ref Reader for UTF8 encoding and default allocator.
  521|       |/*! GenericReader parses JSON text from a stream, and send events synchronously to an
  522|       |    object implementing Handler concept.
  523|       |
  524|       |    It needs to allocate a stack for storing a single decoded string during
  525|       |    non-destructive parsing.
  526|       |
  527|       |    For in-situ parsing, the decoded string is directly written to the source
  528|       |    text string, no temporary buffer is required.
  529|       |
  530|       |    A GenericReader object can be reused for parsing multiple JSON text.
  531|       |
  532|       |    \tparam SourceEncoding Encoding of the input stream.
  533|       |    \tparam TargetEncoding Encoding of the parse output.
  534|       |    \tparam StackAllocator Allocator type for stack.
  535|       |*/
  536|       |template <typename SourceEncoding, typename TargetEncoding, typename StackAllocator = CrtAllocator>
  537|       |class GenericReader {
  538|       |public:
  539|       |    typedef typename SourceEncoding::Ch Ch; //!< SourceEncoding character type
  540|       |
  541|       |    //! Constructor.
  542|       |    /*! \param stackAllocator Optional allocator for allocating stack memory. (Only use for non-destructive parsing)
  543|       |        \param stackCapacity stack capacity in bytes for storing a single decoded string.  (Only use for non-destructive parsing)
  544|       |    */
  545|       |    GenericReader(StackAllocator* stackAllocator = 0, size_t stackCapacity = kDefaultStackCapacity) :
  546|       |        stack_(stackAllocator, stackCapacity), parseResult_(), state_(IterativeParsingStartState) {}
  547|       |
  548|       |    //! Parse JSON text.
  549|       |    /*! \tparam parseFlags Combination of \ref ParseFlag.
  550|       |        \tparam InputStream Type of input stream, implementing Stream concept.
  551|       |        \tparam Handler Type of handler, implementing Handler concept.
  552|       |        \param is Input stream to be parsed.
  553|       |        \param handler The handler to receive events.
  554|       |        \return Whether the parsing is successful.
  555|       |    */
  556|       |    template <unsigned parseFlags, typename InputStream, typename Handler>
  557|      0|    ParseResult Parse(InputStream& is, Handler& handler) {
  558|      0|        if (parseFlags & kParseIterativeFlag)
  559|      0|            return IterativeParse<parseFlags>(is, handler);
  560|      0|
  561|      0|        parseResult_.Clear();
  562|      0|
  563|      0|        ClearStackOnExit scope(*this);
  564|      0|
  565|      0|        SkipWhitespaceAndComments<parseFlags>(is);
  566|      0|        CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR_EARLY_RETURN(parseResult_);
  567|      0|
  568|      0|        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(is.Peek() == '\0')) {
  569|      0|            CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR_NORETURN(kParseErrorDocumentEmpty, is.Tell());
  570|      0|            CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR_EARLY_RETURN(parseResult_);

  573|      0|            ParseValue<parseFlags>(is, handler);
  574|      0|            CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR_EARLY_RETURN(parseResult_);

  576|      0|            if (!(parseFlags & kParseStopWhenDoneFlag)) {
  577|      0|                SkipWhitespaceAndComments<parseFlags>(is);

  581|      0|                    CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR_NORETURN(kParseErrorDocumentRootNotSingular, is.Tell());
  582|      0|                    CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR_EARLY_RETURN(parseResult_);
  583|      0|                }
  584|      0|            }
  585|      0|        }
  586|      0|
  587|      0|        return parseResult_;
  588|      0|    }
  589|       |
  590|       |    //! Parse JSON text (with \ref kParseDefaultFlags)
  591|       |    /*! \tparam InputStream Type of input stream, implementing Stream concept
  592|       |        \tparam Handler Type of handler, implementing Handler concept.
  593|       |        \param is Input stream to be parsed.

  595|       |        \return Whether the parsing is successful.
  596|       |    */
  597|       |    template <typename InputStream, typename Handler>
  598|       |    ParseResult Parse(InputStream& is, Handler& handler) {
  599|       |        return Parse<kParseDefaultFlags>(is, handler);
  600|       |    }
  601|       |
  602|       |    //! Initialize JSON text token-by-token parsing
  603|       |    /*!
  604|       |     */
  605|       |    void IterativeParseInit() {
  606|       |        parseResult_.Clear();
  607|       |        state_ = IterativeParsingStartState;
  608|       |    }
  609|       |
  610|       |    //! Parse one token from JSON text
  611|       |    /*! \tparam InputStream Type of input stream, implementing Stream concept
  612|       |        \tparam Handler Type of handler, implementing Handler concept.
  613|       |        \param is Input stream to be parsed.
  614|       |        \param handler The handler to receive events.
  615|       |        \return Whether the parsing is successful.
  616|       |     */
  617|       |    template <unsigned parseFlags, typename InputStream, typename Handler>
  618|       |    bool IterativeParseNext(InputStream& is, Handler& handler) {
  619|       |        while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(is.Peek() != '\0')) {
  620|       |            SkipWhitespaceAndComments<parseFlags>(is);
  621|       |
  622|       |            Token t = Tokenize(is.Peek());
  623|       |            IterativeParsingState n = Predict(state_, t);
  624|       |            IterativeParsingState d = Transit<parseFlags>(state_, t, n, is, handler);
  625|       |
  626|       |            // If we've finished or hit an error...
  627|       |            if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(IsIterativeParsingCompleteState(d))) {
  628|       |                // Report errors.
  629|       |                if (d == IterativeParsingErrorState) {
  630|       |                    HandleError(state_, is);
  631|       |                    return false;
  632|       |                }
  633|       |
  634|       |                // Transition to the finish state.
  635|       |                CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_ASSERT(d == IterativeParsingFinishState);
  636|       |                state_ = d;
  637|       |
  638|       |                // If StopWhenDone is not set...
  639|       |                if (!(parseFlags & kParseStopWhenDoneFlag)) {
  640|       |                    // ... and extra non-whitespace data is found...
  641|       |                    SkipWhitespaceAndComments<parseFlags>(is);
  642|       |                    if (is.Peek() != '\0') {
  643|       |                        // ... this is considered an error.
  644|       |                        HandleError(state_, is);
  645|       |                        return false;
  646|       |                    }
  647|       |                }
  648|       |
  649|       |                // Success! We are done!
  650|       |                return true;
  651|       |            }
  652|       |
  653|       |            // Transition to the new state.
  654|       |            state_ = d;
  655|       |
  656|       |            // If we parsed anything other than a delimiter, we invoked the handler, so we can return true now.
  657|       |            if (!IsIterativeParsingDelimiterState(n))
  658|       |                return true;
  659|       |        }
  660|       |
  661|       |        // We reached the end of file.
  662|       |        stack_.Clear();
  663|       |
  664|       |        if (state_ != IterativeParsingFinishState) {
  665|       |            HandleError(state_, is);
  666|       |            return false;
  667|       |        }
  668|       |
  669|       |        return true;
  670|       |    }
  671|       |
  672|       |    //! Check if token-by-token parsing JSON text is complete
  673|       |    /*! \return Whether the JSON has been fully decoded.
  674|       |     */
  675|       |    CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_FORCEINLINE bool IterativeParseComplete() const {
  676|       |        return IsIterativeParsingCompleteState(state_);
  677|       |    }
  678|       |
  679|       |    //! Whether a parse error has occurred in the last parsing.
  680|      0|    bool HasParseError() const { return parseResult_.IsError(); }
  681|       |
  682|       |    //! Get the \ref ParseErrorCode of last parsing.
  683|       |    ParseErrorCode GetParseErrorCode() const { return parseResult_.Code(); }
  684|       |
  685|       |    //! Get the position of last parsing error in input, 0 otherwise.
  686|       |    size_t GetErrorOffset() const { return parseResult_.Offset(); }
  687|       |
  688|       |protected:
  689|      0|    void SetParseError(ParseErrorCode code, size_t offset) { parseResult_.Set(code, offset); }
  690|       |

  693|       |    GenericReader(const GenericReader&);

  708|       |    template<unsigned parseFlags, typename InputStream>

  719|      0|                            if (Consume(is, '/'))
  720|      0|                                break;
  721|      0|                        }
  722|      0|                        else
  723|      0|                            is.Take();
  724|      0|                    }
  725|      0|                }
  726|      0|                else if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(Consume(is, '/')))
  727|      0|                    while (is.Peek() != '\0' && is.Take() != '\n') {}
  728|      0|                else

  730|      0|
  731|      0|                SkipWhitespace(is);
  732|      0|            }
  733|      0|        }
  734|      0|    }

  736|       |    // Parse object: { string : value, ... }
  737|       |    template<unsigned parseFlags, typename InputStream, typename Handler>

 1062|       |    // StringStream -> StackStream<char>
 1063|       |    static CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_FORCEINLINE void ScanCopyUnescapedString(StringStream& is, StackStream<char>& os) {
 1064|       |        const char* p = is.src_;
 1065|       |
 1066|       |        // Scan one by one until alignment (unaligned load may cross page boundary and cause crash)
 1067|       |        const char* nextAligned = reinterpret_cast<const char*>((reinterpret_cast<size_t>(p) + 15) & static_cast<size_t>(~15));
 1068|       |        while (p != nextAligned)
 1069|       |            if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(*p == '\"') || CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(*p == '\\') || CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(static_cast<unsigned>(*p) < 0x20)) {
 1070|       |                is.src_ = p;
 1071|       |                return;
 1072|       |            }
 1073|       |            else
 1074|       |                os.Put(*p++);
 1075|       |
 1076|       |        // The rest of string using SIMD
 1077|       |        static const char dquote[16] = { '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"', '\"' };

 1080|       |        const __m128i dq = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&dquote[0]));
 1081|       |        const __m128i bs = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&bslash[0]));
 1082|       |        const __m128i sp = _mm_loadu_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(&space[0]));
 1083|       |
 1084|       |        for (;; p += 16) {
 1085|       |            const __m128i s = _mm_load_si128(reinterpret_cast<const __m128i *>(p));
 1086|       |            const __m128i t1 = _mm_cmpeq_epi8(s, dq);
 1087|       |            const __m128i t2 = _mm_cmpeq_epi8(s, bs);
 1088|       |            const __m128i t3 = _mm_cmpeq_epi8(_mm_max_epu8(s, sp), sp); // s < 0x20 <=> max(s, 0x1F) == 0x1F
 1089|       |            const __m128i x = _mm_or_si128(_mm_or_si128(t1, t2), t3);
 1090|       |            unsigned short r = static_cast<unsigned short>(_mm_movemask_epi8(x));

 1093|       |    #ifdef _MSC_VER         // Find the index of first escaped
 1094|       |                unsigned long offset;
 1095|       |                _BitScanForward(&offset, r);
 1096|       |                length = offset;

 1098|       |                length = static_cast<SizeType>(__builtin_ffs(r) - 1);
 1099|       |    #endif

 1102|       |                    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; i++)
 1103|       |                        q[i] = p[i];
 1104|       |
 1105|       |                    p += length;
 1106|       |                }
 1107|       |                break;
 1108|       |            }
 1109|       |            _mm_storeu_si128(reinterpret_cast<__m128i *>(os.Push(16)), s);
 1110|       |        }
 1111|       |
 1112|       |        is.src_ = p;
 1113|       |    }

 1220|       |    static CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_FORCEINLINE void ScanCopyUnescapedString(StringStream& is, StackStream<char>& os) {
 1221|       |        const char* p = is.src_;
 1222|       |
 1223|       |        // Scan one by one until alignment (unaligned load may cross page boundary and cause crash)
 1224|       |        const char* nextAligned = reinterpret_cast<const char*>((reinterpret_cast<size_t>(p) + 15) & static_cast<size_t>(~15));
 1225|       |        while (p != nextAligned)
 1226|       |            if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(*p == '\"') || CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(*p == '\\') || CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(static_cast<unsigned>(*p) < 0x20)) {
 1227|       |                is.src_ = p;
 1228|       |                return;
 1229|       |            }
 1230|       |            else
 1231|       |                os.Put(*p++);
 1232|       |

 1234|       |        const uint8x16_t s0 = vmovq_n_u8('"');
 1235|       |        const uint8x16_t s1 = vmovq_n_u8('\\');
 1236|       |        const uint8x16_t s2 = vmovq_n_u8('\b');

 1238|       |

 1241|       |            uint8x16_t x = vceqq_u8(s, s0);
 1242|       |            x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, s1));

 1249|       |
 1250|       |            SizeType length = 0;
 1251|       |            bool escaped = false;

 1256|       |                    escaped = true;

 1261|       |                escaped = true;
 1262|       |            }

 1269|       |                    p += length;
 1270|       |                }
 1271|       |                break;
 1272|       |            }

 1274|       |        }
 1275|       |
 1276|       |        is.src_ = p;

 1453|       |    template<unsigned parseFlags, typename InputStream, typename Handler>
 1454|      0|    void ParseNumber(InputStream& is, Handler& handler) {
 1455|      0|        internal::StreamLocalCopy<InputStream> copy(is);
 1456|      0|        NumberStream<InputStream,
 1457|      0|            ((parseFlags & kParseNumbersAsStringsFlag) != 0) ?
 1458|      0|                ((parseFlags & kParseInsituFlag) == 0) :
 1459|      0|                ((parseFlags & kParseFullPrecisionFlag) != 0),
 1460|      0|            (parseFlags & kParseNumbersAsStringsFlag) != 0 &&
 1461|      0|                (parseFlags & kParseInsituFlag) == 0> s(*this, copy.s);
 1462|      0|
 1463|      0|        size_t startOffset = s.Tell();
 1464|      0|        double d = 0.0;
 1465|      0|        bool useNanOrInf = false;
 1466|      0|
 1467|      0|        // Parse minus
 1468|      0|        bool minus = Consume(s, '-');
 1469|      0|
 1470|      0|        // Parse int: zero / ( digit1-9 *DIGIT )
 1471|      0|        unsigned i = 0;
 1472|      0|        uint64_t i64 = 0;
 1473|      0|        bool use64bit = false;
 1474|      0|        int significandDigit = 0;
 1475|      0|        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(s.Peek() == '0')) {
 1476|      0|            i = 0;
 1477|      0|            s.TakePush();
 1478|      0|        }
 1479|      0|        else if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '1' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1480|      0|            i = static_cast<unsigned>(s.TakePush() - '0');
 1481|      0|
 1482|      0|            if (minus)
 1483|      0|                while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1484|      0|                    if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(i >= 214748364)) { // 2^31 = 2147483648
 1485|      0|                        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(i != 214748364 || s.Peek() > '8')) {
 1486|      0|                            i64 = i;
 1487|      0|                            use64bit = true;
 1488|      0|                            break;
 1489|      0|                        }
 1490|      0|                    }
 1491|      0|                    i = i * 10 + static_cast<unsigned>(s.TakePush() - '0');
 1492|      0|                    significandDigit++;
 1493|      0|                }
 1494|      0|            else
 1495|      0|                while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1496|      0|                    if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(i >= 429496729)) { // 2^32 - 1 = 4294967295
 1497|      0|                        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(i != 429496729 || s.Peek() > '5')) {
 1498|      0|                            i64 = i;
 1499|      0|                            use64bit = true;
 1500|      0|                            break;
 1501|      0|                        }
 1502|      0|                    }
 1503|      0|                    i = i * 10 + static_cast<unsigned>(s.TakePush() - '0');
 1504|      0|                    significandDigit++;
 1505|      0|                }
 1506|      0|        }
 1507|      0|        // Parse NaN or Infinity here
 1508|      0|        else if ((parseFlags & kParseNanAndInfFlag) && CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY((s.Peek() == 'I' || s.Peek() == 'N'))) {
 1509|      0|            if (Consume(s, 'N')) {
 1510|      0|                if (Consume(s, 'a') && Consume(s, 'N')) {
 1511|      0|                    d = std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN();
 1512|      0|                    useNanOrInf = true;
 1513|      0|                }
 1514|      0|            }
 1515|      0|            else if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(Consume(s, 'I'))) {
 1516|      0|                if (Consume(s, 'n') && Consume(s, 'f')) {
 1517|      0|                    d = (minus ? -std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity() : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity());
 1518|      0|                    useNanOrInf = true;
 1519|      0|
 1520|      0|                    if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(s.Peek() == 'i' && !(Consume(s, 'i') && Consume(s, 'n')
 1521|      0|                                                                && Consume(s, 'i') && Consume(s, 't') && Consume(s, 'y')))) {
 1522|      0|                        CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR(kParseErrorValueInvalid, s.Tell());
 1523|      0|                    }
 1524|      0|                }
 1525|      0|            }
 1526|      0|
 1527|      0|            if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(!useNanOrInf)) {
 1528|      0|                CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR(kParseErrorValueInvalid, s.Tell());
 1529|      0|            }
 1530|      0|        }
 1531|      0|        else
 1532|      0|            CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR(kParseErrorValueInvalid, s.Tell());
 1533|      0|
 1534|      0|        // Parse 64bit int
 1535|      0|        bool useDouble = false;
 1536|      0|        if (use64bit) {
 1537|      0|            if (minus)
 1538|      0|                while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1539|      0|                     if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(i64 >= CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UINT64_C2(0x0CCCCCCC, 0xCCCCCCCC))) // 2^63 = 9223372036854775808
 1540|      0|                        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(i64 != CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UINT64_C2(0x0CCCCCCC, 0xCCCCCCCC) || s.Peek() > '8')) {
 1541|      0|                            d = static_cast<double>(i64);
 1542|      0|                            useDouble = true;
 1543|      0|                            break;
 1544|      0|                        }
 1545|      0|                    i64 = i64 * 10 + static_cast<unsigned>(s.TakePush() - '0');
 1546|      0|                    significandDigit++;
 1547|      0|                }
 1548|      0|            else
 1549|      0|                while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1550|      0|                    if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(i64 >= CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UINT64_C2(0x19999999, 0x99999999))) // 2^64 - 1 = 18446744073709551615
 1551|      0|                        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(i64 != CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UINT64_C2(0x19999999, 0x99999999) || s.Peek() > '5')) {
 1552|      0|                            d = static_cast<double>(i64);
 1553|      0|                            useDouble = true;
 1554|      0|                            break;
 1555|      0|                        }
 1556|      0|                    i64 = i64 * 10 + static_cast<unsigned>(s.TakePush() - '0');
 1557|      0|                    significandDigit++;
 1558|      0|                }
 1559|      0|        }
 1560|      0|
 1561|      0|        // Force double for big integer
 1562|      0|        if (useDouble) {
 1563|      0|            while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1564|      0|                d = d * 10 + (s.TakePush() - '0');
 1565|      0|            }
 1566|      0|        }
 1567|      0|
 1568|      0|        // Parse frac = decimal-point 1*DIGIT
 1569|      0|        int expFrac = 0;
 1570|      0|        size_t decimalPosition;
 1571|      0|        if (Consume(s, '.')) {
 1572|      0|            decimalPosition = s.Length();
 1573|      0|
 1574|      0|            if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(!(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')))
 1575|      0|                CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR(kParseErrorNumberMissFraction, s.Tell());
 1576|      0|
 1577|      0|            if (!useDouble) {
 1578|      0|#if CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_64BIT
 1579|      0|                // Use i64 to store significand in 64-bit architecture
 1580|      0|                if (!use64bit)
 1581|      0|                    i64 = i;
 1582|      0|
 1583|      0|                while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1584|      0|                    if (i64 > CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UINT64_C2(0x1FFFFF, 0xFFFFFFFF)) // 2^53 - 1 for fast path
 1585|      0|                        break;
 1586|      0|                    else {
 1587|      0|                        i64 = i64 * 10 + static_cast<unsigned>(s.TakePush() - '0');
 1588|      0|                        --expFrac;
 1589|      0|                        if (i64 != 0)
 1590|      0|                            significandDigit++;
 1591|      0|                    }
 1592|      0|                }
 1593|      0|
 1594|      0|                d = static_cast<double>(i64);
 1595|      0|#else
 1596|      0|                // Use double to store significand in 32-bit architecture
 1597|      0|                d = static_cast<double>(use64bit ? i64 : i);
 1598|      0|#endif
 1599|      0|                useDouble = true;
 1600|      0|            }
 1601|      0|
 1602|      0|            while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1603|      0|                if (significandDigit < 17) {
 1604|      0|                    d = d * 10.0 + (s.TakePush() - '0');
 1605|      0|                    --expFrac;
 1606|      0|                    if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(d > 0.0))
 1607|      0|                        significandDigit++;
 1608|      0|                }
 1609|      0|                else
 1610|      0|                    s.TakePush();
 1611|      0|            }
 1612|      0|        }
 1613|      0|        else
 1614|      0|            decimalPosition = s.Length(); // decimal position at the end of integer.
 1615|      0|
 1616|      0|        // Parse exp = e [ minus / plus ] 1*DIGIT
 1617|      0|        int exp = 0;
 1618|      0|        if (Consume(s, 'e') || Consume(s, 'E')) {
 1619|      0|            if (!useDouble) {
 1620|      0|                d = static_cast<double>(use64bit ? i64 : i);
 1621|      0|                useDouble = true;
 1622|      0|            }
 1623|      0|
 1624|      0|            bool expMinus = false;
 1625|      0|            if (Consume(s, '+'))
 1626|      0|                ;
 1627|      0|            else if (Consume(s, '-'))
 1628|      0|                expMinus = true;
 1629|      0|
 1630|      0|            if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1631|      0|                exp = static_cast<int>(s.Take() - '0');
 1632|      0|                if (expMinus) {
 1633|      0|                    // (exp + expFrac) must not underflow int => we're detecting when -exp gets
 1634|      0|                    // dangerously close to INT_MIN (a pessimistic next digit 9 would push it into
 1635|      0|                    // underflow territory):
 1636|      0|                    //
 1637|      0|                    //        -(exp * 10 + 9) + expFrac >= INT_MIN
 1638|      0|                    //   <=>  exp <= (expFrac - INT_MIN - 9) / 10
 1639|      0|                    CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_ASSERT(expFrac <= 0);
 1640|      0|                    int maxExp = (expFrac + 2147483639) / 10;
 1641|      0|
 1642|      0|                    while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1643|      0|                        exp = exp * 10 + static_cast<int>(s.Take() - '0');
 1644|      0|                        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(exp > maxExp)) {
 1645|      0|                            while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9'))  // Consume the rest of exponent
 1646|      0|                                s.Take();
 1647|      0|                        }
 1648|      0|                    }
 1649|      0|                }
 1650|      0|                else {  // positive exp
 1651|      0|                    int maxExp = 308 - expFrac;
 1652|      0|                    while (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_LIKELY(s.Peek() >= '0' && s.Peek() <= '9')) {
 1653|      0|                        exp = exp * 10 + static_cast<int>(s.Take() - '0');
 1654|      0|                        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(exp > maxExp))
 1655|      0|                            CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR(kParseErrorNumberTooBig, startOffset);
 1656|      0|                    }
 1657|      0|                }
 1658|      0|            }
 1659|      0|            else
 1660|      0|                CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR(kParseErrorNumberMissExponent, s.Tell());
 1661|      0|
 1662|      0|            if (expMinus)
 1663|      0|                exp = -exp;
 1664|      0|        }
 1665|      0|
 1666|      0|        // Finish parsing, call event according to the type of number.
 1667|      0|        bool cont = true;
 1668|      0|
 1669|      0|        if (parseFlags & kParseNumbersAsStringsFlag) {
 1670|      0|            if (parseFlags & kParseInsituFlag) {
 1671|      0|                s.Pop();  // Pop stack no matter if it will be used or not.
 1672|      0|                typename InputStream::Ch* head = is.PutBegin();
 1673|      0|                const size_t length = s.Tell() - startOffset;
 1674|      0|                CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_ASSERT(length <= 0xFFFFFFFF);
 1675|      0|                // unable to insert the \0 character here, it will erase the comma after this number
 1676|      0|                const typename TargetEncoding::Ch* const str = reinterpret_cast<typename TargetEncoding::Ch*>(head);
 1677|      0|                cont = handler.RawNumber(str, SizeType(length), false);
 1678|      0|            }
 1679|      0|            else {
 1680|      0|                SizeType numCharsToCopy = static_cast<SizeType>(s.Length());
 1681|      0|                StringStream srcStream(s.Pop());
 1682|      0|                StackStream<typename TargetEncoding::Ch> dstStream(stack_);
 1683|      0|                while (numCharsToCopy--) {
 1684|      0|                    Transcoder<UTF8<>, TargetEncoding>::Transcode(srcStream, dstStream);
 1685|      0|                }
 1686|      0|                dstStream.Put('\0');
 1687|      0|                const typename TargetEncoding::Ch* str = dstStream.Pop();
 1688|      0|                const SizeType length = static_cast<SizeType>(dstStream.Length()) - 1;
 1689|      0|                cont = handler.RawNumber(str, SizeType(length), true);
 1690|      0|            }
 1691|      0|        }
 1692|      0|        else {
 1693|      0|           size_t length = s.Length();
 1694|      0|           const char* decimal = s.Pop();  // Pop stack no matter if it will be used or not.
 1695|      0|
 1696|      0|           if (useDouble) {
 1697|      0|               int p = exp + expFrac;
 1698|      0|               if (parseFlags & kParseFullPrecisionFlag)
 1699|      0|                   d = internal::StrtodFullPrecision(d, p, decimal, length, decimalPosition, exp);
 1700|      0|               else
 1701|      0|                   d = internal::StrtodNormalPrecision(d, p);
 1702|      0|
 1703|      0|               // Use > max, instead of == inf, to fix bogus warning -Wfloat-equal
 1704|      0|               if (d > (std::numeric_limits<double>::max)()) {
 1705|      0|                   // Overflow
 1706|      0|                   // TODO: internal::StrtodX should report overflow (or underflow)
 1707|      0|                   CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR(kParseErrorNumberTooBig, startOffset);
 1708|      0|               }
 1709|      0|
 1710|      0|               cont = handler.Double(minus ? -d : d);
 1711|      0|           }
 1712|      0|           else if (useNanOrInf) {
 1713|      0|               cont = handler.Double(d);
 1714|      0|           }
 1715|      0|           else {
 1716|      0|               if (use64bit) {
 1717|      0|                   if (minus)
 1718|      0|                       cont = handler.Int64(static_cast<int64_t>(~i64 + 1));
 1719|      0|                   else
 1720|      0|                       cont = handler.Uint64(i64);
 1721|      0|               }
 1722|      0|               else {
 1723|      0|                   if (minus)
 1724|      0|                       cont = handler.Int(static_cast<int32_t>(~i + 1));
 1725|      0|                   else
 1726|      0|                       cont = handler.Uint(i);
 1727|      0|               }
 1728|      0|           }
 1729|      0|        }
 1730|      0|        if (CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_UNLIKELY(!cont))
 1731|      0|            CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR(kParseErrorTermination, startOffset);
 1732|      0|    }
 1733|       |
 1734|       |    // Parse any JSON value
 1735|       |    template<unsigned parseFlags, typename InputStream, typename Handler>
 1736|      0|    void ParseValue(InputStream& is, Handler& handler) {
 1737|      0|        switch (is.Peek()) {
 1738|      0|            case 'n': ParseNull  <parseFlags>(is, handler); break;
 1739|      0|            case 't': ParseTrue  <parseFlags>(is, handler); break;
 1740|      0|            case 'f': ParseFalse <parseFlags>(is, handler); break;
 1741|      0|            case '"': ParseString<parseFlags>(is, handler); break;
 1742|      0|            case '{': ParseObject<parseFlags>(is, handler); break;
 1743|      0|            case '[': ParseArray <parseFlags>(is, handler); break;
 1744|      0|            default :
 1745|      0|                      ParseNumber<parseFlags>(is, handler);
 1746|      0|                      break;
 1747|      0|
 1748|      0|        }
 1749|      0|    }

 1801|      0|    CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_FORCEINLINE Token Tokenize(Ch c) const {
 1802|      0|
 1803|      0|//!@cond CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_HIDDEN_FROM_DOXYGEN
 1804|      0|#define N NumberToken
 1805|      0|#define N16 N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N,N
 1806|      0|        // Maps from ASCII to Token
 1807|      0|        static const unsigned char tokenMap[256] = {
 1808|      0|            N16, // 00~0F
 1809|      0|            N16, // 10~1F
 1810|      0|            N, N, StringToken, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, CommaToken, N, N, N, // 20~2F
 1811|      0|            N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, ColonToken, N, N, N, N, N, // 30~3F
 1812|      0|            N16, // 40~4F
 1813|      0|            N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, LeftBracketToken, N, RightBracketToken, N, N, // 50~5F
 1814|      0|            N, N, N, N, N, N, FalseToken, N, N, N, N, N, N, N, NullToken, N, // 60~6F
 1815|      0|            N, N, N, N, TrueToken, N, N, N, N, N, N, LeftCurlyBracketToken, N, RightCurlyBracketToken, N, N, // 70~7F
 1816|      0|            N16, N16, N16, N16, N16, N16, N16, N16 // 80~FF
 1817|      0|        };
 1818|      0|#undef N
 1819|      0|#undef N16
 1820|      0|//!@endcond
 1821|      0|
 1822|      0|        if (sizeof(Ch) == 1 || static_cast<unsigned>(c) < 256)
 1823|      0|            return static_cast<Token>(tokenMap[static_cast<unsigned char>(c)]);
 1824|      0|        else
 1825|      0|            return NumberToken;
 1826|      0|    }

 2176|      0|    ParseResult IterativeParse(InputStream& is, Handler& handler) {
 2177|      0|        parseResult_.Clear();

 2187|      0|

 2189|      0|                HandleError(state, is);
 2190|      0|                break;
 2191|      0|            }

 2194|      0|

 2196|      0|            if ((parseFlags & kParseStopWhenDoneFlag) && state == IterativeParsingFinishState)
 2197|      0|                break;

 2199|      0|            SkipWhitespaceAndComments<parseFlags>(is);
 2200|      0|            CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_PARSE_ERROR_EARLY_RETURN(parseResult_);

 2202|      0|

 2204|      0|        if (state != IterativeParsingFinishState)
 2205|      0|            HandleError(state, is);
 2206|      0|

 2208|      0|    }

   24|       |///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
   25|       |//  Stream
   26|       |
   27|       |/*! \class rapidjson::Stream
   28|       |    \brief Concept for reading and writing characters.
   29|       |
   30|       |    For read-only stream, no need to implement PutBegin(), Put(), Flush() and PutEnd().
   31|       |
   32|       |    For write-only stream, only need to implement Put() and Flush().
   33|       |
   34|       |\code
   35|       |concept Stream {
   36|       |    typename Ch;    //!< Character type of the stream.
   37|       |
   38|       |    //! Read the current character from stream without moving the read cursor.
   39|       |    Ch Peek() const;
   40|       |
   41|       |    //! Read the current character from stream and moving the read cursor to next character.
   42|       |    Ch Take();
   43|       |
   44|       |    //! Get the current read cursor.
   45|       |    //! \return Number of characters read from start.
   46|       |    size_t Tell();
   47|       |
   48|       |    //! Begin writing operation at the current read pointer.
   49|       |    //! \return The begin writer pointer.
   50|       |    Ch* PutBegin();
   51|       |
   52|       |    //! Write a character.
   53|       |    void Put(Ch c);
   54|       |
   55|       |    //! Flush the buffer.
   56|       |    void Flush();
   57|       |
   58|       |    //! End the writing operation.
   59|       |    //! \param begin The begin write pointer returned by PutBegin().
   60|       |    //! \return Number of characters written.
   61|       |    size_t PutEnd(Ch* begin);
   62|       |}
   63|       |\endcode
   64|       |*/
   65|       |
   66|       |//! Provides additional information for stream.
   67|       |/*!
   68|       |    By using traits pattern, this type provides a default configuration for stream.
   69|       |    For custom stream, this type can be specialized for other configuration.
   70|       |    See TEST(Reader, CustomStringStream) in readertest.cpp for example.
   71|       |*/
   72|       |template<typename Stream>
   73|       |struct StreamTraits {
   74|       |    //! Whether to make local copy of stream for optimization during parsing.
   75|       |    /*!
   76|       |        By default, for safety, streams do not use local copy optimization.
   77|       |        Stream that can be copied fast should specialize this, like StreamTraits<StringStream>.
   78|       |    */
   79|       |    enum { copyOptimization = 0 };
   80|       |};
   81|       |
   82|       |//! Reserve n characters for writing to a stream.

   85|      0|    (void)stream;

   92|      0|    stream.Put(c);

  181|       |// InsituStringStream

  183|       |//! A read-write string stream.
  184|       |/*! This string stream is particularly designed for in-situ parsing.
  185|       |    \note implements Stream concept
  186|       |*/
  187|       |template <typename Encoding>
  188|       |struct GenericInsituStringStream {
  189|       |    typedef typename Encoding::Ch Ch;
  190|       |
  191|       |    GenericInsituStringStream(Ch *src) : src_(src), dst_(0), head_(src) {}
  192|       |
  193|       |    // Read
  194|       |    Ch Peek() { return *src_; }
  195|       |    Ch Take() { return *src_++; }
  196|       |    size_t Tell() { return static_cast<size_t>(src_ - head_); }
  197|       |
  198|       |    // Write
  199|       |    void Put(Ch c) { CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_ASSERT(dst_ != 0); *dst_++ = c; }
  200|       |
  201|       |    Ch* PutBegin() { return dst_ = src_; }
  202|       |    size_t PutEnd(Ch* begin) { return static_cast<size_t>(dst_ - begin); }
  203|       |    void Flush() {}
  204|       |
  205|       |    Ch* Push(size_t count) { Ch* begin = dst_; dst_ += count; return begin; }
  206|       |    void Pop(size_t count) { dst_ -= count; }
  207|       |
  208|       |    Ch* src_;
  209|       |    Ch* dst_;
  210|       |    Ch* head_;
  211|       |};

   34|       |//! Represents an in-memory output stream.
   35|       |/*!
   36|       |    \tparam Encoding Encoding of the stream.
   37|       |    \tparam Allocator type for allocating memory buffer.
   38|       |    \note implements Stream concept
   39|       |*/
   40|       |template <typename Encoding, typename Allocator = CrtAllocator>
   41|       |class GenericStringBuffer {
   42|       |public:
   43|       |    typedef typename Encoding::Ch Ch;
   44|       |
   45|       |    GenericStringBuffer(Allocator* allocator = 0, size_t capacity = kDefaultCapacity) : stack_(allocator, capacity) {}
   46|       |
   47|       |#if CEREAL_RAPIDJSON_HAS_CXX11_RVALUE_REFS
   48|       |    GenericStringBuffer(GenericStringBuffer&& rhs) : stack_(std::move(rhs.stack_)) {}
   49|       |    GenericStringBuffer& operator=(GenericStringBuffer&& rhs) {
   50|       |        if (&rhs != this)
   51|       |            stack_ = std::move(rhs.stack_);
   52|       |        return *this;
   53|       |    }
   54|       |#endif
   55|       |
   56|       |    void Put(Ch c) { *stack_.template Push<Ch>() = c; }
   57|      0|    void PutUnsafe(Ch c) { *stack_.template PushUnsafe<Ch>() = c; }
   58|       |    void Flush() {}
   59|       |
   60|       |    void Clear() { stack_.Clear(); }
   61|       |    void ShrinkToFit() {
   62|       |        // Push and pop a null terminator. This is safe.
   63|       |        *stack_.template Push<Ch>() = '\0';
   64|       |        stack_.ShrinkToFit();
   65|       |        stack_.template Pop<Ch>(1);
   66|       |    }
   67|       |
   68|      0|    void Reserve(size_t count) { stack_.template Reserve<Ch>(count); }
   69|      0|    Ch* Push(size_t count) { return stack_.template Push<Ch>(count); }
   70|       |    Ch* PushUnsafe(size_t count) { return stack_.template PushUnsafe<Ch>(count); }
   71|      0|    void Pop(size_t count) { stack_.template Pop<Ch>(count); }
   72|       |
   73|       |    const Ch* GetString() const {
   74|       |        // Push and pop a null terminator. This is safe.
   75|       |        *stack_.template Push<Ch>() = '\0';
   76|       |        stack_.template Pop<Ch>(1);
   77|       |
   78|       |        return stack_.template Bottom<Ch>();
   79|       |    }
   80|       |
   81|       |    //! Get the size of string in bytes in the string buffer.
   82|       |    size_t GetSize() const { return stack_.GetSize(); }
   83|       |
   84|       |    //! Get the length of string in Ch in the string buffer.
   85|       |    size_t GetLength() const { return stack_.GetSize() / sizeof(Ch); }
   86|       |
   87|       |    static const size_t kDefaultCapacity = 256;
   88|       |    mutable internal::Stack<Allocator> stack_;

   90|       |private:
   91|       |    // Prohibit copy constructor & assignment operator.
   92|       |    GenericStringBuffer(const GenericStringBuffer&);
   93|       |    GenericStringBuffer& operator=(const GenericStringBuffer&);
   94|       |};

  636|       |    if (length < 16)

  645|       |    const char* endAligned = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(reinterpret_cast<size_t>(end) & static_cast<size_t>(~15));
  646|       |    if (nextAligned > end)

  659|       |    const uint8x16_t s1 = vmovq_n_u8('\\');
  660|       |    const uint8x16_t s2 = vmovq_n_u8('\b');

  666|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, s1));
  667|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vceqq_u8(s, s2));
  668|       |        x = vorrq_u8(x, vcltq_u8(s, s3));

  671|       |        uint64_t low = vgetq_lane_u64(reinterpret_cast<uint64x2_t>(x), 0);   // extract

  673|       |
  674|       |        SizeType len = 0;

  676|       |        if (low == 0) {
  677|       |            if (high != 0) {
  678|       |                unsigned lz = (unsigned)__builtin_clzll(high);
  679|       |                len = 8 + (lz >> 3);
  680|       |                escaped = true;
  681|       |            }

  684|       |            len = lz >> 3;
  685|       |            escaped = true;

   14|       |template<typename Dst, typename Src>
   15|       |inline Dst safe_cast(const Src value)
   16|      0|{
   17|      0|  typedef std::numeric_limits<Dst> DstLim;
   18|      0|  typedef std::numeric_limits<Src> SrcLim;
   19|      0|
   20|      0|  const bool positive_overflow_possible = safe_less(
   21|      0|    DstLim::max(),
   22|      0|    SrcLim::max()
   23|      0|  );
   24|      0|  const bool negative_overflow_possible =
   25|      0|      SrcLim::is_signed
   26|      0|      or
   27|      0|      safe_greater(DstLim::lowest(), SrcLim::lowest());
   28|      0|
   29|      0|  // unsigned <-- unsigned
   30|      0|  if((not DstLim::is_signed) and (not SrcLim::is_signed)) {

   32|      0|      throw std::overflow_error(__PRETTY_FUNCTION__ +
   33|      0|                    std::string(": positive overflow"));
   34|      0|    }
   35|      0|  }
   36|      0|  // unsigned <-- signed
   37|      0|  else if((not DstLim::is_signed) and SrcLim::is_signed) {
   38|      0|    if(positive_overflow_possible and safe_greater(value, DstLim::max())) {
   39|      0|      throw std::overflow_error(__PRETTY_FUNCTION__ +
   40|      0|                    std::string(": positive overflow"));
   41|      0|    }

   63|       |template<typename BottomFrac = std::ratio<1, 2> >
   64|       |size_t sidebyside_hash(const size_t top, const size_t bottom) {
   65|       |
   66|       |  constexpr size_t int_size = sizeof(int) * 8;
   67|       |  // exclude int sign bit
   68|       |  constexpr size_t avail_size = int_size - 1;
   69|       |  constexpr size_t bottom_size = avail_size * BottomFrac::num / BottomFrac::den;
   70|       |  [[maybe_unused]] constexpr size_t top_size = avail_size - bottom_size;
   71|       |
   72|       |  // bounds checking
   73|       |  uitsl_assert(std::bitset<top_size>(top).to_ullong() == top, top);
   74|       |  uitsl_assert(std::bitset<bottom_size>(bottom).to_ullong() == bottom, bottom);
   75|       |
   76|       |  const auto bottom_bits = std::bitset<int_size>( bottom );
   77|       |  const auto top_bits = std::bitset<int_size>( top ) << bottom_size;
   78|       |
   79|       |  const auto res = top_bits | bottom_bits;
   80|       |
   81|       |  return res.to_ullong();
   82|       |
   83|       |}

   59|       |  /// Implementation of the murmur3 hash, a fast hash with low collisions.
   60|       |  /// This hash makes it suitable for hash-based lookups.
   61|       |  /// For more info, see: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MurmurHash
   62|       |  /// This implementation was directly based on:
   63|       |  /// https://github.com/aappleby/smhasher/blob/92cf3702fcfaadc84eb7bef59825a23e0cd84f56/src/MurmurHash3.cpp
   64|       |  /// @param key Span of bytes to hash.
   65|       |  /// @param seed Optional seed.
   66|       |  /// @return Hash of key.
   67|       |  constexpr inline size_t murmur_hash(
   68|       |    const std::span<const std::byte> key,
   69|       |    const size_t seed = 0
   70|      0|  ) {
   71|      0|    // define constants
   72|      0|    const size_t numbytes = key.size();
   73|      0|    const size_t nblocks = numbytes / 16;
   74|      0|    const uint64_t c1 = 0x87c37b91114253d5LLU;
   75|      0|    const uint64_t c2 = 0x4cf5ad432745937fLLU;
   76|      0|
   77|      0|    // initialize seeds
   78|      0|    uint64_t h1 = seed;
   79|      0|    uint64_t h2 = seed;
   80|      0|
   81|      0|    // helper lambda for reading std::bytes into uint64_ts
   82|      0|    const auto get_ints = [&key](const size_t idx) {
   83|      0|      uint64_t ret{0};
   84|      0|      std::memcpy(&ret, key.data() + idx * sizeof(uint64_t), sizeof(uint64_t));
   85|      0|      return ret;
   86|      0|    };
   87|      0|
   88|      0|    // main algorithm loop
   89|      0|    // does not run if length < 16
   90|      0|    for (size_t i = 0; i < nblocks; i++) {
   91|      0|      uint64_t k1 = get_ints(2 * i);
   92|      0|      uint64_t k2 = get_ints(2 * i + 1);
   93|      0|
   94|      0|      k1 *= c1;
   95|      0|      k1 = internal::rotate(k1, 31);
   96|      0|      k1 *= c2;
   97|      0|      h1 ^= k1;
   98|      0|
   99|      0|      h1 = internal::rotate(h1, 27);
  100|      0|      h1 += h2;
  101|      0|      h1 = 5 * h1 + 0x52dce729;
  102|      0|
  103|      0|      k2 *= c2;
  104|      0|      k2 = internal::rotate(k2, 33);
  105|      0|      k2 *= c1;
  106|      0|      h2 ^= k2;
  107|      0|
  108|      0|      h2 = internal::rotate(h2, 31);
  109|      0|      h2 += h1;
  110|      0|      h2 = 5 * h2 + 0x38495ab5;
  111|      0|    }
  112|      0|    // tail of algorithm
  113|      0|    uint64_t k1 = 0;
  114|      0|    uint64_t k2 = 0;
  115|      0|
  116|      0|    // helper lambda for reading std::bytes as uint64_ts and then shifting them left
  117|      0|    const auto do_magic = [&key, nblocks](const size_t a, const size_t b) {
  118|      0|      uint64_t tail_{0};
  119|      0|      std::memcpy(&tail_, key.data() + nblocks*16 + a, sizeof(uint8_t));
  120|      0|      return tail_ << b;
  121|      0|    };
  122|      0|
  123|      0|    switch(numbytes & 15) {
  124|      0|      case 15: k2 ^= do_magic(14, 48);
  125|      0|      case 14: k2 ^= do_magic(13, 40);
  126|      0|      case 13: k2 ^= do_magic(12, 32);
  127|      0|      case 12: k2 ^= do_magic(11, 24);
  128|      0|      case 11: k2 ^= do_magic(10, 16);
  129|      0|      case 10: k2 ^= do_magic(9, 8);
  130|      0|      case  9: k2 ^= do_magic(8, 0);
  131|      0|               k2 *= c2;
  132|      0|               k2  = internal::rotate(k2,33);
  133|      0|               k2 *= c1;
  134|      0|               h2 ^= k2;
  135|      0|      case  8: k1 ^= do_magic(7, 56);
  136|      0|      case  7: k1 ^= do_magic(6, 48);
  137|      0|      case  6: k1 ^= do_magic(5, 40);
  138|      0|      case  5: k1 ^= do_magic(4, 32);
  139|      0|      case  4: k1 ^= do_magic(3, 24);
  140|      0|      case  3: k1 ^= do_magic(2, 16);
  141|      0|      case  2: k1 ^= do_magic(1, 8);

  146|      0|               h1 ^= k1;
  147|      0|    };

  149|      0|    // finalization
  150|      0|    h1 ^= numbytes;
  151|      0|    h2 ^= numbytes;
  152|      0|
  153|      0|    h1 += h2;

  166|       |  /// This structure serves as a hash for containers that are iterable.

  272|       |    /// Generate a random variable drawn from a unit normal distribution.
  273|      0|    double GetRandNormal() {
  274|      0|      // Draw from a Unit Normal Dist
  275|      0|      // Using Rejection Method and saving of initial exponential random variable
  276|      0|      double expRV2;
  277|      0|      while (1) {
  278|      0|        expRV2 = -log(GetDouble());
  279|      0|        expRV -= (expRV2-1)*(expRV2-1)/2;
  280|      0|        if (expRV > 0) break;
  281|      0|        expRV = -log(GetDouble());
  282|      0|      }
  283|      0|      if (P(.5)) return expRV2;
  284|      0|      return -expRV2;
  285|      0|    }

  292|       |    /// @return A random variable drawn from a pareto distribution.
  293|       |    /// @param alpha Shape parameter (default 1).
  294|       |    /// @param lower_bound Lower bound (default 1).
  295|       |    /// @param upper_bound Upper bound (default infinity, unbounded).
  296|       |    /// See <https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pareto_distribution>
  297|       |    inline double GetRandPareto(
  298|       |      const double alpha=1.0,
  299|       |      const double lower_bound=1.0,
  300|       |      const double upper_bound=std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity()
  301|      0|    ) {
  302|      0|      emp_assert( alpha > 0.0, alpha );
  303|      0|      emp_assert( lower_bound > 0.0, lower_bound );
  304|      0|      emp_assert( lower_bound <= upper_bound, lower_bound, upper_bound );
  305|      0|      if (lower_bound == upper_bound) return lower_bound;
  306|      0|      // uses inverse transform sampling method
  307|      0|      // see https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Inverse_transform_sampling
  308|      0|      // and https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pareto_distribution
  309|      0|
  310|      0|      // see https://gist.github.com/mmore500/b6de0158a8851ce6e3d65105ed35197f
  311|      0|      // for validation and testing
  312|      0|      // url has also been archived on the archive.org wayback machine
  313|      0|
  314|      0|      const double unif_lb = std::pow(lower_bound / upper_bound, alpha);
  315|      0|      constexpr double unif_ub = 1.0;
  316|      0|      const double unif_sample = GetDouble(unif_lb, unif_ub);
  317|      0|
  318|      0|      return lower_bound / std::pow(unif_sample, 1.0/alpha);
  319|      0|    }

  337|       |    /// @return A random variable drawn from a distribution with symmetric
  338|       |    /// pareto tails extending both positively and negatively from zero.
  339|       |    /// @param alpha Shape parameter (default 1).
  340|       |    /// @param lambda Scale parameter (default 1).
  341|       |    /// @param lower_bound Lower bound (default -infinity, unbounded).
  342|       |    /// @param upper_bound Upper bound (default infinity, unbounded).
  343|       |    /// Inspired by <https://doi.org/10.1109/TBC.2004.834013>
  344|       |    inline double GetRandZeroSymmetricPareto(
  345|       |      const double alpha=1.0,
  346|       |      const double lambda=1.0,
  347|       |      const double lower_bound=-std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity(),
  348|       |      const double upper_bound=std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity()
  349|      0|    ) {
  350|      0|      emp_assert( alpha > 0.0, alpha );
  351|      0|      emp_assert( lower_bound <= 0, lower_bound );
  352|      0|      emp_assert( upper_bound >= 0, upper_bound );
  353|      0|
  354|      0|      // see https://gist.github.com/mmore500/2b95ef3279e49fc1780b3b319c35083a
  355|      0|      // for derivations and validation
  356|      0|      // url has also been archived on the archive.org wayback machine
  357|      0|
  358|      0|      const double n = std::min(std::abs(lower_bound), upper_bound);
  359|      0|      const double m = std::max(std::abs(lower_bound), upper_bound);
  360|      0|
  361|      0|      // probability of picking on the skinny side of zero
  362|      0|      const double p_skinny_side
  363|      0|        = impl::calc_p_zero_symmetric_pareto_skinny_side(
  364|      0|          alpha, lambda, m, n
  365|      0|        );
  366|      0|
  367|      0|      // is the fat side of the distribution negative?
  368|      0|      const bool skinny_side_is_positive = std::abs(lower_bound) > upper_bound;
  369|      0|
  370|      0|      // pick which side of zero to draw from then do draw with a lomax dist
  371|      0|      if (P(p_skinny_side) == skinny_side_is_positive) {
  372|      0|        // draw from positive side
  373|      0|        return GetRandLomax(alpha, lambda, upper_bound);
  374|      0|      } else {
  375|      0|        // draw from negative side
  376|      0|        return -GetRandLomax(alpha, lambda, std::abs(lower_bound));
  377|      0|      }
  378|      0|
  379|      0|    }

  390|      0|    inline uint32_t GetRandPoisson(const double mean) {
  391|      0|      // Draw from a Poisson Dist with mean; if cannot calculate, return UINT_MAX.
  392|      0|      // Uses Rejection Method

  394|      0|      if (a <= 0) return UINT_MAX; // cannot calculate, so return UINT_MAX
  395|      0|      uint32_t k = 0;
  396|      0|      double u = GetDouble();
  397|      0|      while (u >= a) {
  398|      0|        u *= GetDouble();
  399|      0|        ++k;
  400|      0|      }
  401|      0|      return k;
  402|      0|    }

   37|       |    }

   55|       |
   56|       |    /// Produce a vector that spreads values evenly across the range.
   57|       |    emp::vector<T> Spread(size_t s) const {
   58|       |      emp_assert(s >= 1);
   59|       |      emp::vector<T> out(s);
   60|       |      out[0] = lower;
   61|       |      if (s > 1) {
   62|       |        T range = upper - lower;
   63|       |        for (size_t i = 1; i < s; i++) {
   64|       |          out[i] = lower + (T) ((((double) i) / (double)(s-1)) * range);
   65|       |        }
   66|       |      }
   67|       |      return out;
   68|       |    }
   69|       |  };

  218|       |  /// A fast (O(log p)) integral-power command.
  219|       |  template <typename T>
  220|       |  static constexpr T PowIntImpl(T base, T p) {
  221|       |    if (p <= 0) return 1;
  222|       |    if (p & 1) return base * PowIntImpl(base, p-1); // Odd exponent: strip one mulitple off and recurse.
  223|       |    return emp::Square( PowIntImpl(base,p/2) );          // Even exponent: calc for half and square result.
  224|       |  }
  225|       |  } // namespace internal
  226|       |  #endif // DOXYGEN_SHOULD_SKIP_THIS

  242|       |  /// A fast method for calculating exponents on doubles.
  243|      0|  static constexpr double PowDoubleImpl(double base, double exp) {
  244|      0|    // Normally, convert to a base of 2 and then use Pow2.
  245|      0|    // If base is negative, we don't want to deal with imaginary numbers, so use IntPow.
  246|      0|    return (base > 0)
  247|      0|      ? emp::Pow2(emp::Log2(base) * exp)
  248|      0|      : emp::IntPow(base,exp);
  249|      0|  }

  258|       |  /// A fast method for calculating exponents on doubles or integral types.
  259|       |  /// Uses if constexpr to work around compiler bug in Emscripten (issue #296).
  260|       |  template<typename T>
  261|       |  static constexpr T Pow(
  262|       |    T base, typename internal::identity<T>::type exp
  263|       |  ) {
  264|       |    // TODO cpp20, C++20 replace with std::is_constant_evaluated
  265|       |    // adapted from https://stackoverflow.com/a/62610143
  266|       |    // exclude clang versions with compiler bug https://reviews.llvm.org/D35190
  267|       |    #if defined(__clang__) && __clang_major__>=9 || defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__)
  268|       |    // if base is not known at compile time, use std::pow which is faster
  269|       |    if ( !__builtin_constant_p( base ) ) return std::pow(base, exp);
  270|       |    // otherwise, use constexpr-friendly implementations
  271|       |    else
  272|       |    #endif
  273|       |    if constexpr( std::is_integral<T>::value ){
  274|       |      return internal::PowIntImpl(base, exp);
  275|       |    } else return internal::PowDoubleImpl(base, exp);
  276|       |  }

  299|       |  /// A version of Min that allows a variable number of inputs to be compared.
  300|       |  template <typename T> const T & Min(std::initializer_list<const T&> lst) {
  301|       |    emp_assert(lst.size > 0); // Nothing to return if nothing in the list!
  302|       |    auto min_found = lst.begin();
  303|       |    for (auto it = lst.begin()+1; it < lst.end(); it++) {
  304|       |      if (*it < *min_found) min_found = it;
  305|       |    }
  306|       |    return *min_found;
  307|       |  }

  314|       |      if (*it > *max_found) max_found = it;
  315|       |    }

  372|      0|
  373|      0|    union {
