/home/runner/workspace/third-party/conduit/third-party/Empirical/include/emp/meta/TypePack.hpp:
    1|       |/**
    2|       | *  @note This file is part of Empirical, https://github.com/devosoft/Empirical
    3|       | *  @copyright Copyright (C) Michigan State University, MIT Software license; see doc/LICENSE.md
    4|       | *  @date 2016-2018
    5|       | *
    6|       | *  @file  TypePack.hpp
    7|       | *  @brief A set of types that can be manipulated at compile time (good for metaprogramming)
    8|       | *
    9|       | *  TypePacks are static structues that provide a large set of mechanisms to access and adjust
   10|       | *  the included types.
   11|       | *
   12|       | *  To create a typepack, just pass in zero or more types into the TypePack template.
   13|       | *
   14|       | *    using my_pack = emp::TypePack<int, std::string, double>;
   15|       | *
   16|       | *  After manipulations, you can apply a type pack using the apply<> member template.  E.g.,
   17|       | *
   18|       | *    my_pack::resize<5,char>::reverse::apply<std::tuple> my_tuple;
   19|       | *
   20|       | *  ...would create a variable of type std::tuple<char, char, double, std::string, int>.
   21|       | *
   22|       | *
   23|       | *  Member functions include (all of which are constexpr):
   24|       | *    Has<T>()           - Return true/false: Is T is part of the pack?
   25|       | *    Count<T>()         - Return number of times T is in the pack.
   26|       | *    GetID<T>()         - Return first position of T in the pack, (or -1 if none).
   27|       | *    GetSize()          - Return total number of types in this pack.
   28|       | *    IsEmpty()          - Return true/false: Is this pack empty?
   29|       | *    IsUnique()         - Return true/false: are all types in pack are distinct?
   30|       | *
   31|       | *  Type accessors:
   32|       | *    get<POS>           - Type at position POS in the pack.
   33|       | *    first_t            - Type of first position in the pack.
   34|       | *    last_t             - Type of last position in the pack.
   35|       | *    select<Ps...>      - Create a new pack with types from selected positions.
   36|       | *
   37|       | *  Type manipulations:
   38|       | *    set<POS, T>        - Change type at position POS to T.
   39|       | *    push_front<Ts...>  - Add any number of types Ts to the front of the pack.
   40|       | *    push_back<Ts...>   - Add any number of types Ts to the back of the pack.
   41|       | *    pop                - Pack with first type missing.
   42|       | *    popN<N>            - Pack with first N types missing.
   43|       | *    shrink<N>          - Pack with ONLY first N types.
   44|       | *    resize<N,D>        - Resize pack to N types; if N greater than current size, pad with D.
   45|       | *    merge<P>           - Append all of pack P to the end of this pack.
   46|       | *    find_union<P>      - Join this pack to P, keeping only one of each type.
   47|       | *    reverse            - Reverse the order of types in this pack.
   48|       | *    rotate             - Move the first type in pack to the end.
   49|       | *
   50|       | *  Applications:
   51|       | *    apply<T>           - Take template T and apply these types as its arguments.
   52|       | *    to_function_t<T>   - Convert to a function type, with return type T and arg types from pack.
   53|       | *    filter<FILTER>     - Keep only those types, T, that can legally form FILTER<T> and does not
   54|       | *                         have a FILTER<T>::value == false.
   55|       | *    filter_out<FILTER> - Remove those types, T, that can legally form FILTER<T> and do not have
   56|       | *                         a FILTER<T>::value == false.
   57|       | *    find<FILTER>       - Convert to first type, T, that can legally form FILTER<T> and does not
   58|       | *                         have a FILTER<T>::value == false.
   59|       | *    wrap<WRAPPER>      - Convert to TypePack where all members are run through WRAPPER
   60|       | *
   61|       | *
   62|       | *  Developer notes:
   63|       | *    - GetIDPack could return an ValPack of ALL ID's for a type that appears more than once.
   64|       | */
   65|       |
   66|       |#ifndef EMP_TYPE_PACK_H
   67|       |#define EMP_TYPE_PACK_H
   68|       |
   69|       |#include "meta.hpp"
   70|       |
   71|       |namespace emp {
   72|       |
   73|       |  // Pre-declaration of TypePack
   74|       |  template <typename... Ts> struct TypePack;
   75|       |
   76|       |  // Create a null type for padding.
   77|       |  struct null_t {};
   78|       |
   79|       |  // Private helpers for TypePack
   80|       |  #ifndef DOXYGEN_SHOULD_SKIP_THIS
   81|       |  namespace internal {
   82|       |    // Create add N copies of the same type to the end of a TypePack.
   83|       |    template <typename START, typename T, int N>
   84|       |    struct tp_pad { using type = typename tp_pad<START,T,N-1>::type::template add<T>; };
   85|       |    template <typename START, typename T>
   86|       |    struct tp_pad<START, T,0> { using type = START; };
   87|       |
   88|       |    /// Helper for shifting a specified number of types to TypePack T1 from TypePack T2.
   89|       |    /// Example: to shrink, move the specified number of types to empty TypePack and return it.
   90|       |    ///          to merge, move all of one TypePack over to the other and return it.
   91|       |    template <int S, typename T1, typename T2>
   92|       |    struct tp_shift {
   93|       |      using move_t = typename T2::first_t;
   94|       |      using inc_t = typename T1::template add<move_t>;
   95|       |      using dec_t = typename T2::pop;
   96|       |      using type1 = typename tp_shift<S-1, inc_t, dec_t>::type1;
   97|       |      using type2 = typename tp_shift<S-1, inc_t, dec_t>::type2;
   98|       |    };
   99|       |    template <typename T1, typename T2>
  100|       |    struct tp_shift<0, T1, T2> {
  101|       |      using type1 = T1;
  102|       |      using type2 = T2;
  103|       |    };
  104|       |
  105|       |    // Filters create a TypePack with the element if the filter is true, empty if false.
  106|       |    template <typename T, bool> struct tp_filter1 { using type=TypePack<T>; };
  107|       |    template <typename T> struct tp_filter1<T,false> { using type=TypePack<>; };
  108|       |
  109|       |    /// Remove types from a TypePack that can't satisfy a filter.
  110|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class FILTER, int N>
  111|       |    struct tp_filter {
  112|       |      using cur_t = typename T::first_t;                                 // Isolate the first type
  113|       |      using other_tp = typename T::pop;                                  // Isolate remaining types
  114|       |      constexpr static bool cur_result = test_type<FILTER,cur_t>();      // Run filter of cur type
  115|       |      using cur_ftp = typename tp_filter1<cur_t, cur_result>::type;      // Use cur type if true
  116|       |      using other_ftp = typename tp_filter<other_tp, FILTER, N-1>::type; // Recurse
  117|       |      using type = typename cur_ftp::template merge< other_ftp >;        // Merge
  118|       |    };
  119|       |
  120|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class FILTER>
  121|       |      struct tp_filter<T,FILTER,0> { using type = TypePack<>; };
  122|       |
  123|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class FILTER>
  124|       |      using tp_filter_t = typename tp_filter<T,FILTER,T::SIZE>::type;
  125|       |
  126|       |    /// Remove types from a TypePack that DO satisfy a filter.
  127|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class FILTER, int N>
  128|       |    struct tp_filter_out {
  129|       |      using cur_t = typename T::first_t;                                 // Isolate the first type
  130|       |      using other_tp = typename T::pop;                                  // Isolate remaining types
  131|       |      constexpr static bool cur_result = test_type<FILTER,cur_t>();      // Run filter of cur type
  132|       |      using cur_ftp = typename tp_filter1<cur_t, !cur_result>::type;     // Use cur type if true
  133|       |      using other_ftp = typename tp_filter_out<other_tp, FILTER, N-1>::type; // Recurse
  134|       |      using type = typename cur_ftp::template merge< other_ftp >;        // Merge
  135|       |    };
  136|       |
  137|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class FILTER>
  138|       |      struct tp_filter_out<T,FILTER,0> { using type = TypePack<>; };
  139|       |
  140|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class FILTER>
  141|       |      using tp_filter_out_t = typename tp_filter_out<T,FILTER,T::SIZE>::type;
  142|       |
  143|       |    // Wrappers create a TypePack with the wrapped element if the filter is true, empty if false.
  144|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class W, bool> struct tp_wrap1
  145|       |      { using type=TypePack<W<T>>; };
  146|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class W> struct tp_wrap1<T,W,false>
  147|       |      { using type=TypePack<>; };
  148|       |
  149|       |    /// Wrap all types in a TypePack in a template; remove any that can't be wrapped.
  150|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class W, int N> struct tp_wrap {
  151|       |      using cur_t = typename T::first_t;                               // Isolate the first type
  152|       |      using other_tp = typename T::pop;                                // Isolate remaining types
  153|       |      constexpr static bool cur_result = test_type<W,cur_t>();         // Use wrap to filter cur type
  154|       |      using cur_ftp = typename tp_wrap1<cur_t, W, cur_result>::type;   // Use cur type if true
  155|       |      using other_ftp = typename tp_wrap<other_tp, W, N-1>::type;      // Recurse
  156|       |      using type = typename cur_ftp::template merge< other_ftp >;      // Merge
  157|       |    };
  158|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class W>
  159|       |      struct tp_wrap<T,W,0> { using type = TypePack<>; };
  160|       |
  161|       |    template <typename T, template <typename...> class W>
  162|       |      using tp_wrap_t = typename tp_wrap<T,W,T::SIZE>::type;
  163|       |
  164|       |    // Tools to build all combinations of types from a TypePack
  165|       |    template <size_t size,
  166|       |              typename ALL_OPTIONS,
  167|       |              typename CUR_OPTIONS>
  168|       |    struct all_combos {
  169|       |      // Determine combinations for one level smaller.
  170|       |      using next_combos = typename all_combos<size-1, ALL_OPTIONS, ALL_OPTIONS>::result_t;
  171|       |
  172|       |      using cur_type = typename CUR_OPTIONS::first_t;
  173|       |      using next_options = typename CUR_OPTIONS::pop;
  174|       |      template <typename T>
  175|       |      using push_wrap = typename T::template push_back<cur_type>;
  176|       |
  177|       |      // pushed_types is the set of currently-built types with the FIRST option added on.
  178|       |      using pushed_types = typename next_combos::template wrap<push_wrap>;
  179|       |
  180|       |      // other_results is the set of currently built types with the OTHER options added on.
  181|       |      using other_results = typename all_combos<size, ALL_OPTIONS, next_options>::result_t;
  182|       |
  183|       |      // cur_result_t is the merger of pushed_type and other types (i.e., ALL types added on)
  184|       |      using result_t = typename pushed_types::template merge<other_results>;
  185|       |    };
  186|       |
  187|       |    // When we are out of options return an empty pack.
  188|       |    template <size_t size,
  189|       |              typename ALL_OPTIONS>
  190|       |    struct all_combos<size, ALL_OPTIONS, emp::TypePack<>> {
  191|       |      using result_t = emp::TypePack<>;
  192|       |    };
  193|       |
  194|       |    // If we are out of combinations, just return the current answer.
  195|       |    template <typename ALL_OPTIONS>
  196|       |    struct all_combos<0,ALL_OPTIONS,ALL_OPTIONS> {
  197|       |      using result_t = emp::TypePack<emp::TypePack<>>;
  198|       |    };
  199|       |  }
  200|       |  #endif // DOXYGEN_SHOULD_SKIP_THIS
  201|       |
  202|       |  template <typename T, int N>
  203|       |  using TypePackFill = typename internal::tp_pad<TypePack<>,T,N>::type;
  204|       |
  205|       |  // Specialized TypePack with at least one type.
  206|       |  template <typename T1, typename... Ts>
  207|       |  struct TypePack<T1, Ts...> {
  208|       |    /// Return a bool indicating whether the specified type is present.
  209|       |    template <typename T> constexpr static bool Has() { return has_type<T,T1,Ts...>(); }
  210|       |
  211|       |    /// Count the number of instances of the specified type.
  212|       |    template <typename T> constexpr static size_t Count() { return count_type<T,T1,Ts...>(); }
  213|       |
  214|       |    /// Return the position of the specified type.
  215|       |    template <typename T> constexpr static int GetID() { return get_type_index<T,T1,Ts...>(); }
  216|       |
  217|       |    /// Return the position of the type of owner.
  218|       |    template <typename T>
  219|       |    constexpr static int GetID(const T &) { return get_type_index<T,T1,Ts...>(); }
  220|       |
  221|       |    /// Set to the number of types in this pack.
  222|       |    constexpr static int SIZE = 1+sizeof...(Ts);
  223|       |
  224|       |    /// Return the number of types in this pack.
  225|       |    constexpr static int GetSize() { return SIZE; }
  226|       |
  227|       |    /// Return bool indicating if there are any types in this pack.
  228|       |    constexpr static bool IsEmpty() { return false; }
  229|       |
  230|       |    /// Return bool indicating if all types in this pack are different from each other.
  231|       |    constexpr static bool IsUnique() { return has_unique_types<T1,Ts...>(); }
  232|       |
  233|       |    /// Count how many distinct types are in this pack.
  234|       |    constexpr static int CountUnique() { return make_unique::GetSize(); }
  235|       |
  236|       |    /// Get the type associated with a specified position in the pack.
  237|       |    template <int POS> using get = pack_id<POS, T1, Ts...>;
  238|       |
  239|       |    // Other type lookups
  240|       |
  241|       |    /// What is the full type of this TypePack?
  242|       |    // (mostly a helper for the code below)
  243|       |    using this_t = TypePack<T1, Ts...>;
  244|       |
  245|       |    /// What is the first type in this TypePack?
  246|       |    using first_t = T1;
  247|       |
  248|       |    /// What is the final type in this TypePack?
  249|       |    using last_t = last_type<T1,Ts...>;
  250|       |
  251|       |    // Modifications
  252|       |
  253|       |    /// Push a new type onto the front of this TypePack.
  254|       |    template <typename... T> using push_front = TypePack<T...,T1,Ts...>;
  255|       |
  256|       |    /// Push a new type onto the back of this TypePack.
  257|       |    template <typename... T> using push_back = TypePack<T1,Ts...,T...>;
  258|       |
  259|       |    /// Push a new type onto the back of this TypePack.
  260|       |    template <typename... T> using add = TypePack<T1,Ts...,T...>;    // Same as push_back_t...
  261|       |
  262|       |    /// Remove the first type from this TypePack and return the rest.
  263|       |    using pop = TypePack<Ts...>;
  264|       |    template <int N> using popN = typename internal::tp_shift<N, TypePack<>, this_t>::type2;
  265|       |
  266|       |    /// Reduce the size of this TypePack down to N.
  267|       |    template <int N> using shrink = typename internal::tp_shift<N, TypePack<>, this_t>::type1;
  268|       |
  269|       |    /// Add N new entries onto TypePack, all of provided type T.
  270|       |    template <typename T, int N=1> using pad = typename internal::tp_pad<this_t,T,N>::type;
  271|       |
  272|       |    /// Make this TypePack the specified size, padding with provided type T.
  273|       |    template <int N, typename DEFAULT=null_t>
  274|       |      using resize = typename pad<DEFAULT,(N>SIZE)?(N-SIZE):0>::template shrink<N>;
  275|       |
  276|       |    /// Join this TypePack with another TypePack (append)
  277|       |    template <typename IN> using merge = typename internal::tp_shift<IN::SIZE, this_t, IN>::type1;
  278|       |
  279|       |    /// Join this TypePack with another, keeping only one of each type.
  280|       |    template <typename IN> using find_union = typename internal::tp_shift<IN::SIZE, this_t, IN>::type1::make_unique;
  281|       |
  282|       |    /// Rearrange types in TypePack into reverse order.
  283|       |    using reverse = typename pop::reverse::template push_back<T1>;
  284|       |
  285|       |    /// Rotate types through typepack by the specified number of steps.
  286|       |    using rotate = typename pop::template push_back<T1>;
  287|       |
  288|       |    /// Set the type at the specified position to the new type provided.  Return as new TypePack.
  289|       |    template <int ID, typename T>
  290|       |      using set = typename shrink<ID>::template push_back<T>::template merge<popN<ID+1>>;
  291|       |
  292|       |    /// Choose a set of specific positions.  Return as new TypePack.
  293|       |    template <int... Ps> using select = TypePack< get<Ps>... >;
  294|       |
  295|       |    /// Convert to a function signature with a specified return type.
  296|       |    template <typename RETURN_T> using to_function_t = RETURN_T(T1,Ts...);
  297|       |
  298|       |    /// Apply to a specified template with TypePack as template arguments.
  299|       |    template <template <typename...> class TEMPLATE> using apply = TEMPLATE<T1, Ts...>;
  300|       |
  301|       |    /// Remove all types that cannot pass a filter.  Return as new TypePack.
  302|       |    template <template <typename...> class FILTER>
  303|       |    using filter = internal::tp_filter_t<this_t, FILTER>;
  304|       |
  305|       |    /// Remove all types that DO pass a filter.  Return as new TypePack.
  306|       |    template <template <typename...> class FILTER>
  307|       |    using filter_out = internal::tp_filter_out_t<this_t, FILTER>;
  308|       |
  309|       |    /// Return the first type that satisfies a filter.
  310|       |    template <template <typename...> class FILTER>
  311|       |    using find_t = typename internal::tp_filter_t<this_t, FILTER>::first_t;
  312|       |
  313|       |    template <typename M1>
  314|       |    struct type_matcher {
  315|       |      template <typename M2>
  316|       |      using is_match = std::is_same<M1,M2>;
  317|       |    };
  318|       |
  319|       |    /// Remove all instances of a given type.
  320|       |    template <typename T>
  321|       |    using remove_t = internal::tp_filter_out_t<this_t, type_matcher<T>::template is_match>;
  322|       |
  323|       |    /// Remove all duplicates within a TypePack
  324|       |    using make_unique = typename remove_t<first_t>::make_unique::template push_front<first_t>;
  325|       |
  326|       |    /// Wrap all types in a specified wrapper template.
  327|       |    template <template <typename...> class WRAPPER>
  328|       |    using wrap = internal::tp_wrap_t<this_t, WRAPPER>;
  329|       |
  330|       |    /// Figure out all of the combinations of types possible.
  331|       |    template <size_t COMBO_SIZE>
  332|       |    using make_combos = typename internal::all_combos<COMBO_SIZE,this_t,this_t>::result_t;
  333|       |  };
  334|       |
  335|       |  // Specialized TypePack with no types.
  336|       |  template <>
  337|       |  struct TypePack<> {
  338|       |    template <typename T> constexpr static bool Has() { return false; }
  339|       |    template <typename T> constexpr static size_t Count() { return 0; }
  340|       |
  341|       |    // GetID() NOT IMPLEMENTED since no ID's are available.
  342|       |    constexpr static int SIZE = 0;
  343|      0|    constexpr static int GetSize() { return 0; }
  344|      0|    constexpr static bool IsEmpty() { return true; }
  345|      0|    constexpr static bool IsUnique() { return true; }
  346|       |
  347|       |    // pop_t not implemented, since no types are available.
  348|       |
  349|       |    using this_t = TypePack<>;
  350|       |    using first_t = null_t;
  351|       |    using last_t = null_t;
  352|       |
  353|       |    template <typename... T> using push_front = TypePack<T...>;
  354|       |    template <typename... T> using push_back = TypePack<T...>;
  355|       |    template <typename... T> using add = TypePack<T...>;
  356|       |
  357|       |    // In both of these cases, N had better be zero... but no easy way to assert.
  358|       |    template <int N> using popN = this_t;
  359|       |    template <int N> using shrink = this_t;
  360|       |
  361|       |    template <typename T, int N=1> using pad = typename internal::tp_pad<this_t,T,N>::type;
  362|       |    template <int N, typename DEFAULT=null_t> using resize = pad<DEFAULT,N>;
  363|       |
  364|       |    template <typename IN> using merge = IN;
  365|       |    using reverse = this_t;
  366|       |    using rotate = this_t;
  367|       |
  368|       |    template <typename RETURN_T> using to_function_t = RETURN_T();
  369|       |
  370|       |    template <template <typename...> class TEMPLATE> using apply = TEMPLATE<>;
  371|       |
  372|       |    // There's nothing to filter, so return this_t (TypePack<>) or null_t
  373|       |    template <template <typename...> class FILTER> using filter = this_t;
  374|       |    template <template <typename...> class FILTER> using filter_out = this_t;
  375|       |    template <template <typename...> class FILTER> using find_t = null_t;
  376|       |    template <typename T> using remove_t = this_t;
  377|       |    using make_unique = this_t;
  378|       |    template <template <typename...> class WRAPPER> using wrap = this_t;
  379|       |  };
  380|       |
  381|       |  /// A type trait to determine if a template that we are looking at is some kind of type pack.
  382|       |  template <typename T> struct is_TypePack : std::false_type {};
  383|       |  template <typename... Ts> struct is_TypePack<emp::TypePack<Ts...>> : std::true_type {};
  384|       |}
  385|       |
  386|       |/// Macro for building a TypePack from a set of types.
  387|      0|#define EMP_BUILD_TYPE_PACK(...) emp::TypePack<__VA_ARGS__>
  388|       |
  389|       |#endif

  802|       |//
  803|       |// [views.span] - A view over a contiguous, single-dimension sequence of objects
  804|       |//
  805|       |template< class T, extent_t Extent /*= dynamic_extent*/ >
  806|       |class span
  807|       |{
  808|       |public:
  809|       |    // constants and types
  810|       |
  811|       |    typedef T element_type;
  812|       |    typedef typename std11::remove_cv< T >::type value_type;
  813|       |
  814|       |    typedef T &       reference;
  815|       |    typedef T *       pointer;
  816|       |    typedef T const * const_pointer;
  817|       |    typedef T const & const_reference;
  818|       |
  819|       |    typedef size_t    size_type;
  820|       |    typedef extent_t  extent_type;
  821|       |
  822|       |    typedef pointer        iterator;
  823|       |    typedef const_pointer  const_iterator;
  824|       |
  825|       |    typedef std::ptrdiff_t difference_type;
  826|       |
  827|       |    typedef std::reverse_iterator< iterator >       reverse_iterator;
  828|       |    typedef std::reverse_iterator< const_iterator > const_reverse_iterator;
  829|       |
  830|       |//    static constexpr extent_type extent = Extent;
  831|       |    enum { extent = Extent };
  832|       |
  833|       |    // 26.7.3.2 Constructors, copy, and assignment [span.cons]
  834|       |
  835|       |#if span_HAVE( DEFAULT_FUNCTION_TEMPLATE_ARG )
  836|       |    span_REQUIRES_0(
  837|       |        ( Extent == 0 ) ||
  838|       |        ( Extent == dynamic_extent )
  839|       |    )
  840|       |#endif
  841|       |    span_constexpr span() span_noexcept
  842|       |        : data_( span_nullptr )
  843|       |        , size_( 0 )
  844|       |    {
  845|       |        // span_EXPECTS( data() == span_nullptr );
  846|       |        // span_EXPECTS( size() == 0 );
  847|       |    }
  848|       |
  849|       |    span_constexpr_exp span( pointer ptr, size_type count )
  850|       |        : data_( ptr )
  851|       |        , size_( count )
  852|       |    {
  853|       |        span_EXPECTS(
  854|       |            ( ptr == span_nullptr && count == 0 ) ||
  855|       |            ( ptr != span_nullptr && count >= 0 )
  856|       |        );
  857|       |    }
  858|       |
  859|       |    span_constexpr_exp span( pointer firstElem, pointer lastElem )
  860|       |        : data_( firstElem )
  861|       |        , size_( to_size( std::distance( firstElem, lastElem ) ) )
  862|       |    {
  863|       |        span_EXPECTS(
  864|       |            std::distance( firstElem, lastElem ) >= 0
  865|       |        );
  866|       |    }
  867|       |
  868|       |    template< std::size_t N
  869|       |#if span_HAVE( DEFAULT_FUNCTION_TEMPLATE_ARG )

  871|       |            (Extent == dynamic_extent || Extent == static_cast<extent_t>(N))
  872|       |            && std::is_convertible< value_type(*)[], element_type(*)[] >::value

  993|       |    // 26.7.3.3 Subviews [span.sub]
  994|       |
  995|       |    template< extent_type Count >
  996|       |    span_constexpr_exp span< element_type, Count >
  997|       |    first() const
  998|       |    {
  999|       |        span_EXPECTS( 0 <= Count && Count <= size() );
 1000|       |
 1001|       |        return span< element_type, Count >( data(), Count );
 1002|       |    }
 1003|       |
 1004|       |    template< extent_type Count >
 1005|       |    span_constexpr_exp span< element_type, Count >
 1006|       |    last() const
 1007|       |    {
 1008|       |        span_EXPECTS( 0 <= Count && Count <= size() );
 1009|       |
 1010|       |        return span< element_type, Count >( data() + (size() - Count), Count );
 1011|       |    }
 1012|       |
 1013|       |#if span_HAVE( DEFAULT_FUNCTION_TEMPLATE_ARG )
 1014|       |    template< size_type Offset, extent_type Count = dynamic_extent >
 1015|       |#else
 1016|       |    template< size_type Offset, extent_type Count /*= dynamic_extent*/ >
 1017|       |#endif
 1018|       |    span_constexpr_exp span< element_type, Count >
 1019|       |    subspan() const
 1020|       |    {
 1021|       |        span_EXPECTS(
 1022|       |            ( 0 <= Offset && Offset <= size() ) &&
 1023|       |            ( Count == dynamic_extent || (0 <= Count && Count + Offset <= size()) )
 1024|       |        );
 1025|       |
 1026|       |        return span< element_type, Count >(
 1027|       |            data() + Offset, Count != dynamic_extent ? Count : (Extent != dynamic_extent ? Extent - Offset : size() - Offset) );
 1028|       |    }
 1029|       |
 1030|       |    span_constexpr_exp span< element_type, dynamic_extent >
 1031|       |    first( size_type count ) const
 1032|       |    {
 1033|       |        span_EXPECTS( 0 <= count && count <= size() );
 1034|       |
 1035|       |        return span< element_type, dynamic_extent >( data(), count );
 1036|       |    }
 1037|       |
 1038|       |    span_constexpr_exp span< element_type, dynamic_extent >
 1039|       |    last( size_type count ) const
 1040|       |    {
 1041|       |        span_EXPECTS( 0 <= count && count <= size() );
 1042|       |
 1043|       |        return span< element_type, dynamic_extent >( data() + ( size() - count ), count );
 1044|       |    }
 1045|       |
 1046|       |    span_constexpr_exp span< element_type, dynamic_extent >
 1047|       |    subspan( size_type offset, size_type count = static_cast<size_type>(dynamic_extent) ) const
 1048|       |    {
 1049|       |        span_EXPECTS(
 1050|       |            ( ( 0 <= offset  && offset <= size() ) ) &&
 1051|       |            ( count == static_cast<size_type>(dynamic_extent) || ( 0 <= count && offset + count <= size() ) )
 1052|       |        );
 1053|       |
 1054|       |        return span< element_type, dynamic_extent >(
 1055|       |            data() + offset, count == static_cast<size_type>(dynamic_extent) ? size() - offset : count );
 1056|       |    }

 1096|       |        return *( data() + idx );
 1097|       |    }

 1621|       |{
 1622|       |#if span_HAVE( STATIC_ASSERT )
 1623|       |    static_assert( Extent != nonstd::dynamic_extent && I < Extent, "get<>(span): dynamic extent or index out of range" );
 1624|       |#endif
 1625|       |    return spn[I];
 1626|       |}
 1627|       |
 1628|       |} // end namespace std
 1629|       |
 1630|       |#endif // span_HAVE( STRUCT_BINDING )
 1631|       |
 1632|       |#if ! span_USES_STD_SPAN
 1633|       |span_RESTORE_WARNINGS()
 1634|       |#endif  // span_USES_STD_SPAN
 1635|       |
 1636|       |#endif  // NONSTD_SPAN_HPP_INCLUDED
